|u(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{M}{1+t},\text{ for any }t>0\text{ and }x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}.

By choosing
M
sufficiently small such that
M\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g
, we obtain

(1+t)|u(t,x)|\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g.

This completes the proof of the linear-in-time decay estimate for the integral (
5.11
), and hence establishes the corresponding linear decay for the homogeneous solutions
\mathscr{E}^{l+1}_{\textup{hom},i}
and
\mathscr{B}^{l+1}_{\textup{hom},i}
.

5.2.2.
Decay Estimates for
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})_{\pm,ib1}
and
(\mathscr{B}^{l+1})_{\pm,ib1}

Now we consider the relativistic radiation contribution
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})_{\pm,ib1}
and
(\mathscr{B}^{l+1})_{\pm,ib1}
from the initial data
f^{l+1}_{\pm}(0,\cdot,\cdot).

Recall that

(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)=\pm\int_{\partial B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\frac{dS_{y}}{|y-x|}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\ \left((\delta_{ij})^{\top}_{i=1,2,3}-\frac{(\omega+\hat{v}_{\pm})(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{j}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\right)\omega^{j}f^{l+1}_{\pm}(0,y,v),

with the standard Einstein summation convention. We aim to prove linear-in-
t
decay of the boundary integral expression

\sum_{\pm}(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)

assuming
f^{l+1}_{\pm}(0,y,v)
decays fast in both
y
and
v
, and where

\omega=\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
is the outward unit normal at the sphere of radius
t
, and

\hat{v}_{\pm}=\frac{v}{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}
.
As in (
5.21
), we follow the notation

K_{ij}^{(\pm)}(w,v)\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega+\hat{v}_{\pm})(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{j}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\right)\omega^{j}.

By the estimate (
5.27
), we obtain that

|K_{ij}^{(\pm)}(w,\hat{v}_{\pm})|\lesssim\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}.

Note that
|y-x|=t
, since
y\in\partial B(x;t)
, and hence

|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{1}{t}\int_{\partial B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}dS_{y}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}|f^{l+1}_{\pm}(0,y,v)|.

Recall that the initial perturbation
f^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}
satisfies the decay assumption (
2.15
) and hence

|f^{l+1}_{\pm}(0,y,v)|\lesssim e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}v^{0}_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta y_{3}}.

Then, we obtain

(5.15)

|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{1}{t}\frac{\langle m_{\pm}\rangle}{\beta^{4}m_{\pm}}\int_{\partial B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}dS_{y}\,e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta y_{3}},

since

(5.16)

\begin{split}&\int_{{\mathbb{R}^{3}}}dv\ v^{0}_{\pm}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}v^{0}_{\pm}}=\int_{{\mathbb{R}^{3}}}dv\ \sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}\\
&=4\pi\int_{0}^{\infty}d|v|\ |v|^{2}\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}=4\pi\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{|v|d|v|}{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}\ |v|(m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2})e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}\\
&=4\pi\int_{m_{\pm}}^{\infty}dz\ \sqrt{z^{2}-m_{\pm}^{2}}z^{2}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}z}\leq 4\pi\int_{0}^{\infty}dz\ z^{3}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}z}=\frac{1024\pi}{\beta^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}dz^{\prime}\ z^{\prime 3}e^{-z^{\prime}}\approx\frac{1}{\beta^{4}},\end{split}

where we made the change of variables
|v|\mapsto z\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}
and then made another change of variables
z\mapsto z^{\prime}\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\frac{\beta}{4}z
.
Note that on
\partial B(x;t)
we have
|x-y|=t.
We consider the surface integral

(5.17)

I\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\int_{\partial B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}dS_{y}\,e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta y_{3}},

where
y=x+t\omega
, and
\omega=(\sin\theta\cos\phi,\sin\theta\sin\phi,\cos\theta)
. Then we have

y_{\parallel}=x_{\parallel}+t\omega_{\parallel}=(x_{1}+t\sin\theta\cos\phi,\;x_{2}+t\sin\theta\sin\phi),\quad y_{3}=x_{3}+t\cos\theta.

We compute the pointwise bound for the integrand:

\displaystyle|y_{\parallel}|^{2}

\displaystyle=|x_{\parallel}+t\omega_{\parallel}|^{2}=|x_{\parallel}|^{2}+2t\,x_{\parallel}\cdot\omega_{\parallel}+t^{2}\sin^{2}\theta

\displaystyle=|x_{\parallel}|^{2}+2t\sin\theta(x_{1}\cos\phi+x_{2}\sin\phi)+t^{2}\sin^{2}\theta.

Since
x_{1}\cos\phi+x_{2}\sin\phi\geq-|x_{\parallel}|
, we have

|y_{\parallel}|^{2}\geq(t\sin\theta-|x_{\parallel}|)^{2},\text{ and hence }|y_{\parallel}|\geq\left|t\sin\theta-|x_{\parallel}|\right|.

Therefore,

(5.18)

e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\beta}{2}\left|t\sin\theta-|x_{\parallel}|\right|\right),

and

e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta y_{3}}=e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta(x_{3}+t\cos\theta)}.

Therefore, the full integrand in spherical coordinates is bounded as

\displaystyle t^{2}\sin\theta\,e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta y_{3}}

\displaystyle\leq t^{2}\sin\theta\,\exp\left(-\frac{\beta}{2}\left|t\sin\theta-|x_{\parallel}|\right|\right)\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta(x_{3}+t\cos\theta)},

and the full integral (
5.17
) is bounded by

I\leq\int_{0}^{2\pi}d\phi\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}d\theta\,t^{2}\sin\theta\,\exp\left(-\frac{\beta}{2}\left|t\sin\theta-|x_{\parallel}|\right|\right)e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta(x_{3}+t\cos\theta)}\\
\leq 2\pi\int_{0}^{1}dk\ t^{2}e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta(x_{3}+tk)}\leq\frac{4\pi t}{m_{\pm}g\beta}e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta x_{3}}\left(1-e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta t}\right),

where we made a change of variables
\theta\mapsto k\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\cos\theta
.

Putting it all together, under the decay condition (
2.15
) of the initial data, we obtain for any
t\geq 0
and
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+},

(5.19)

|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{\langle m_{\pm}\rangle}{m_{\pm}^{2}g\beta^{5}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta x_{3}}\left(1-e^{-\frac{1}{2}m_{\pm}g\beta t}\right).

One can further improve this bound (
5.19
) to a linearly decaying in time upper bound estimate by using the compact-support-in-
x
and decay-in-
v
assumption.
In this case, by the estimate (
5.15
), we have

(5.20)

|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{\langle m_{\pm}\rangle}{\beta^{4}m_{\pm}}\frac{1}{t}\int_{\partial B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}dS_{y}\,\mathscr{M}(y).

Now, define the following integral:

\bar{u}(t,x)\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\frac{1}{4\pi t}\int_{\partial B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}dS_{y}\,\mathscr{M}(y).

Note that this integral
\bar{u}(t,x)
is the same as
u(t,x)
of (
5.13
) if the integrand

\left(t\,\mathscr{B}^{1}_{01}(y)+\mathscr{B}_{01}(y)+\nabla\mathscr{B}_{01}(y)\cdot(y-x)\right)

in (
5.13
) is now replaced by
t\mathscr{M}(y).
Then the same estimate can be made for
\bar{u}
as that of
u(t,x)
in Section
5.2.1
, since
t\mathscr{M}(y)
is also assumed to be compactly supported. Thus, by (
5.14
), we have

|\bar{u}(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}|\mathscr{M}(y)|}{1+t}.

Therefore, by (
5.20
), we obtain

|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{\langle m_{\pm}\rangle}{\beta^{4}m_{\pm}}\frac{\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}|\mathscr{M}(y)|}{1+t}.

By choosing
\beta\gg 1
sufficiently large such that

\frac{\langle m_{\pm}\rangle}{\beta^{4}m_{\pm}}\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}|\mathscr{M}(y)|\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g,

we obtain

|(1+t)(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g.

Here note that we do not need the smallness on
\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}|\mathscr{M}(y)|
, as we can choose
\beta
sufficiently large in this case.

Remark 5.5

(Under the Initial Radiation Charge Neutrality Condition)
.

The compact-support-in-
x
assumption can be replaced by the following weaker condition, called the initial relativistic radiation charge neutrality condition:

(5.21)

\sup_{t\geq 1,\ x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\left|\int_{|x-y|=t}dS_{y}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\left(K_{ij}^{(+)}f_{+}(0,y,v)-K_{ij}^{(-)}f_{-}(0,y,v)\right)\right|\leq\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}\frac{g}{128},

where
K_{ij}^{(\pm)}
denotes the relativistic projection tensor associated with each species, defined by

K_{ij}^{(\pm)}(w,v)\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{i})(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{j}}{1+\omega\cdot\hat{v}_{\pm}},

where
\hat{v}_{\pm}=\frac{v}{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}
denotes the normalized relativistic velocity, and
\omega=\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
is a fixed reference direction. This condition prevents the emergence of unbounded transverse field components arising from the initial charge imbalance and is essential to closing the nonlinear decay estimates.

In this scenario, for
t\in[0,1],
we further obtain from (
5.19
) that

\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}(1+t)|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|\bigg|_{t\in[0,1]}\lesssim\frac{\langle m_{\pm}\rangle}{m_{\pm}^{2}g\beta^{5}}.

Choosing sufficiently large
\beta>0
such that
\frac{\langle m_{\pm}\rangle}{\beta^{5}m_{\pm}^{2}g}\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g,
we have

(1+t)|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|\bigg|_{t\in[0,1]}\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g.

On the other hand, if
t\geq 1,
we use the initial relativistic radiation charge neutrality condition (
5.21
) to obtain

|\sum_{\pm}(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|\bigg|_{t\geq 1}\leq\frac{1}{t}\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}\frac{g}{128}\leq\frac{1}{(1+t)}\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}\frac{g}{64}.

Therefore, we observe that
|\sum_{\pm}(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}(t,x)|
is decaying linearly in time under the additional neutrality assumption (
5.21
).

The same estimates also hold for
(\mathscr{B}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib1}
and
(\mathscr{B}^{l+1})^{(2)}_{\pm,ib1}
as well as
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(2)}_{\pm,ib1}
, as long as we have similar kernel estimates with the same upper-bound (up to constant). In the rest of the proof, we make the kernel estimates.

In general, we will first have an upper bound of the kernel
\left|\frac{(|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{\pm}+\omega)}{(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)^{2}}\right|
in terms of
v.

Note that if
\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega\geq-\delta
for some constant
\delta\in[-1,1),
then we have

\left|\frac{(|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{\pm}+\omega)}{(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)^{2}}\right|\leq 2(1-\delta)^{-2}.

Indeed the term
1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega
is singular at
\omega=-\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}
and this is the worst-case scenario in terms of the upper-bound estimates. At
\omega=-\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|},
we observe that the singularity cancels out as

\left|\frac{\hat{v}_{\pm}+\omega}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\bigg|_{\omega=-\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}}\right|=\left|\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})-\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}\right|=1.

On the other hand, observe that we have another cancellation

(5.22)

\left|\frac{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}-1}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\right|\leq\left|\frac{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}-1}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\bigg|_{\omega=-\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}}\right|=\left|\frac{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}-1}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}\right|=\left|1+|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\right|\leq 2.

In order to see that
\frac{\hat{v}_{\pm}+\omega}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}
is not singular for any
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
, we decompose the sphere
\mathbb{S}^{2}
around the vector
z\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}-\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}
and consider the decomposition of polar angle
\phi\in[0,\pi]
into
[0,\epsilon)
and
[\epsilon,\pi]
such that

\omega\cdot z=-\frac{\omega\cdot(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}=\cos\phi.

Then we observe that a further orthogonal decomposition gives

(5.23)

\frac{\hat{v}_{\pm}+\omega}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}=\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})+(\omega\cdot\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|})\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}+\omega_{\perp}}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi}=\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})-\cos\phi\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}+\omega_{\perp}}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi},

where
\omega_{\perp}\cdot(\hat{v}_{\pm})=0.
Then if
\phi\in[0,\epsilon),
we have

\left|\frac{\omega_{\perp}}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi}\right|\leq\frac{|\sin\phi|}{|1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi|}\leq\frac{\epsilon}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}.

On the other hand, if
\phi\in[\epsilon,\pi],
we have

\left|\frac{\omega_{\perp}}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi}\right|\leq\frac{1}{|1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\epsilon|}=\frac{1}{|1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|+2|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\sin^{2}(\frac{\epsilon}{2})|}.

Indeed, we let
\sin\phi=x
and find the maximal value of

f(\sin\phi)=\frac{|\sin\phi|}{|1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi|}
at the critical point
x
for
\phi\in\ [0,\pi/2].
Note that

f^{\prime}(x)=\frac{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\sqrt{1-x^{2}}-x^{2}\frac{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}}{(1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\sqrt{1-x^{2}})^{2}}=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|(1-x^{2})-x^{2}|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}(1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\sqrt{1-x^{2}})^{2}}\\
=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}(1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\sqrt{1-x^{2}})^{2}}.

It becomes zero when
x=\sqrt{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}}.
Then the maximal value for
F_{\pm}
is

(5.24)

f(x)\leq f(\sqrt{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}})=\frac{\sqrt{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}}}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left|\frac{v}{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}\right|^{2}}}=\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}.

We also have

(5.25)

\left|\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})-\cos\phi\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi}\right|\leq|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\left|\frac{1-\cos\phi\frac{1}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi}\right|\leq|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|+|\cos\phi|\left|\frac{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}}{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi}\right|\\
\leq|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|+|1+|(\hat{v}_{\pm})||\leq 3.

Altogether we conclude that for any
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}

(5.26)

\left|\frac{(|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{\pm}+\omega)}{(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)^{2}}\right|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}=\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}.

Then, for the magnetic field, by using (
5.23
)–(
5.25
) again, we have

(5.27)

\left|\left((\delta_{ij})^{\top}_{i=1,2,3}-\frac{(\omega+\hat{v}_{\pm})(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{j}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\right)\omega^{j}\right|\leq 1+\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}\leq 2\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}.

On the other hand, regarding the electric field, we define

a^{\mathbf{E}}_{\pm,i}(v,\omega)=\frac{(\partial_{v_{i}}v-(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{i}(\hat{v}_{\pm}))}{(v^{0}_{\pm})(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{i})(\omega-(\omega\cdot(\hat{v}_{\pm}))(\hat{v}_{\pm}))}{(v^{0}_{\pm})(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)^{2}}\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}a^{(1)}_{\pm,i}+a^{(2)}_{\pm,i}.

We need to have an upper bound of the kernel
|a_{i}^{E}|.
For
a^{(2)}_{\pm,i},
we use (
5.23
)–(
5.25
) and obtain first

(5.28)

\frac{\omega+(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{(v^{0}_{\pm})(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)}\leq\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}.

Then note that

\omega-(\omega\cdot(\hat{v}_{\pm}))(\hat{v}_{\pm})=\left(\omega\cdot\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}\right)\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}+\omega_{\perp}-(\omega\cdot(\hat{v}_{\pm}))(\hat{v}_{\pm})=-\cos\phi\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}+\omega_{\perp}+\cos\phi|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|(\hat{v}_{\pm})

following the orthogonal decomposition as in (
5.23
). Thus,

|\omega-(\omega\cdot(\hat{v}_{\pm}))(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\leq\left|\cos\phi\frac{\hat{v}_{\pm}}{|\hat{v}_{\pm}|}(|\hat{v}_{\pm}|^{2}-1)\right|+|\omega_{\perp}|\leq|\cos\phi||(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}-1|+|\sin\phi|.

Thus, following the bounds (
5.24
), we have

\frac{|\omega-(\omega\cdot(\hat{v}_{\pm}))(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}{(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)}\leq\frac{|\cos\phi||(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}-1|+|\sin\phi|}{(1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi)}\leq(1+|\hat{v}_{\pm}|)+\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}\leq 3\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}.

Together with (
5.28
), we have

(5.29)

|a^{(2)}_{\pm,i}|\leq\frac{3\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}^{2}}.

Now, regarding
a^{(1)}_{\pm,i}
, a simple calculation gives

\frac{(\partial_{v_{i}}v-(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{i}(\hat{v}_{\pm}))}{(v^{0}_{\pm})(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)}\leq\frac{2}{(v^{0}_{\pm})(1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|)}=\frac{2}{\left(\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}-|v|\right)}=\frac{2}{m_{\pm}^{2}}\left(\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}+|v|\right)\\
\leq\frac{4}{m_{\pm}^{2}}\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}.

Thus, we have

(5.30)

|a^{(1)}_{\pm,i}|\leq\frac{4\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}^{2}}.

This completes the estimates for
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})_{\pm,ib1}
and
(\mathscr{B}^{l+1})_{\pm,ib1}
.

5.2.3.
Decay Estimates for
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})_{\pm,ib2}

Lastly, we consider the contribution
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})_{\pm,ib2}
from the boundary profile
f^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)
at
x_{3}=0
for the electric field. To obtain desired estimates, we have to estimate the following term

(5.31)

(0,0,1)^{\top}-\frac{(\omega+\hat{v}_{\pm})(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\textup{ and }(0,0,1)^{\top}-\frac{(\bar{\omega}+(\hat{v}_{\pm}))(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}}{1+(\hat{v}_{\pm})\cdot\bar{\omega}},

where
\bar{\omega}=(\omega_{1},\omega_{2},-\omega_{3})^{\top}.
For each, we use (
5.23
)–(
5.25
) (and the latter one with
\bar{w}
replacing
w
) and obtain that in both cases we have

(5.32)

\left|(0,0,1)^{\top}-\frac{(\omega+\hat{v}_{\pm})(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\right|,\ \left|(0,0,1)^{\top}-\frac{(\bar{\omega}+(\hat{v}_{\pm}))(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}}{1+(\hat{v}_{\pm})\cdot\bar{\omega}}\right|\leq 1+|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}.

Since the perturbation
f^{l+1}_{\pm}
from the steady-state satisfies the zero inflow boundary condition for the inflow direction
v_{3}\geq 0
at
x_{3}=0,
we will obtain the following upper bound for
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib2}
and
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(2)}_{\pm,ib2}
via the following kernel estimates for (
5.31
)-(
5.32
) and the decay estimate (
5.10
):

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle\left(|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib2}(t,x)|+|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(2)}_{\pm,ib2}(t,x)|\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}=0\}}\frac{dy_{\parallel}}{|y-x|}\int_{v_{3}\leq 0}dv\ \left(1+|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\right)\langle t\rangle|f^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-|x-y|,y_{\parallel},0,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}=0\}}\frac{dy_{\parallel}}{|y-x|}\int_{v_{3}\leq 0}dv\ \frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}v^{0}_{\pm}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{\pm,\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}}{\beta m_{\pm}}\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}=0\}}\frac{dy_{\parallel}}{|y-x|}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|},

where
c_{\pm,\beta}\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\int_{{\mathbb{R}^{3}}}dv\ v^{0}_{\pm}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}v^{0}_{\pm}}\lesssim\beta^{-4}
by (
5.16
) and
C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}
is defined by (
5.2.4
).
We split the integral region
B(x;t)
into two:
|x-y|<1
and
|x-y|\geq 1.
If
|x-y|\geq 1,
then the following inequality holds uniformly:

\langle t\rangle\leq\sqrt{2}\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|.

Therefore, we obtain that if
|x-y|\geq 1,

\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}=0\}\cap|x-y|\geq 1}\frac{dy_{\parallel}}{|y-x|}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}dy_{\parallel}\ e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}\lesssim 1.

On the other hand, if
|x-y|<1,
we further note that

\langle t\rangle\leq\sqrt{2}\langle t-|x-y|\rangle\langle|x-y|\rangle,
and also note that
|x-y|=\sqrt{|x_{\parallel}-y_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}}
if
y_{3}=0.
Then we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}=0\}\cap|x-y|<1}\frac{dy_{\parallel}}{|y-x|}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\sqrt{|x_{\parallel}-y_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}}<\min\{1,t\}}\frac{dy_{\parallel}}{\sqrt{|x_{\parallel}-y_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}}}\left\langle\sqrt{|x_{\parallel}-y_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}}\right\rangle e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\sqrt{|x_{\parallel}-y_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}}<\min\{1,t\}}\frac{dy_{\parallel}}{|x_{\parallel}-y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{dy_{\parallel}}{|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}\approx\frac{1}{\beta}\left\langle\frac{\beta}{2}x_{\parallel}\right\rangle^{-1}\lesssim\frac{1}{\beta},

by (
4.16
). Therefore, we conclude that

\langle t\rangle\left(|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib2}(t,x)|+|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(2)}_{\pm,ib2}(t,x)|\right)\lesssim\frac{C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}}{\beta^{5}m_{\pm}}(1+\beta^{-1}),

by (
5.16
). If
\beta>1
is chosen sufficiently large such that
\min\{m_{-}^{2},m_{+}^{2}\}g\beta^{5}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}\gg 1
, then we have

\langle t\rangle\left(|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib2}(t,x)|+|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(2)}_{\pm,ib2}(t,x)|\right)\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g.

This completes the estimates for
|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,ib2}(t,x)|
and
|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(2)}_{\pm,ib2}(t,x)|
boundary contribution terms.

5.2.4.
Decay Estimates for
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})_{\pm,iS}

One of the main challenges in establishing temporal decay estimates for
\mathscr{E}^{l+1}
lies in handling the nonlinear term
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{\textup{acc}}_{\pm,iS}
and the inhomogeneous stationary source term
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{\textup{st}}_{\pm,iS}
. Our strategy is to control the nonlinear term
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{\textup{acc}}_{\pm,iS}
by using the linear-in-time decay estimate for
f^{l+1}_{\pm}
established in Section
5.2
, together with the uniform boundedness of the total fields
\mathbf{E}^{l}
and
\mathbf{B}^{l}
provided by the bootstrap assumption (
5.7
) and the steady-state estimate (
2.11
). For the inhomogeneous stationary source term
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{\textup{st}}_{\pm,iS}
, we employ the linear decay-in-time estimates for the perturbations
\mathscr{E}^{l}
and
\mathscr{B}^{l}
from (
5.7
).

Namely, we observe that

(5.33)

\langle t\rangle|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1),\textup{acc}}_{\pm,iS}(t,x)|\leq\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,|a^{\mathbf{E}}_{\pm,i}(v,\omega)||\pm\mathbf{E}^{l}\pm\hat{v}_{\pm}\times\mathbf{B}^{l}-m_{\pm}g\hat{e}_{3}|\frac{\langle t\rangle f^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-|x-y|,y,v)}{|x-y|}\\
\leq\frac{63}{8}m_{\pm}g\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}^{2}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})},

by the kernel estimates (
5.29
)–(
5.30
), the decay estimate for
f^{l+1}_{\pm}
(
5.10
), and the uniform bounds for
\mathbf{E}_{\textup{st}},

\mathbf{B}_{\textup{st}},

\mathscr{E}^{l},
and
\mathscr{B}^{l}
in (
2.11
) and (
5.7
) where we define

C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\|\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}f^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}\frac{g}{8}\|\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}(\cdot,0,\cdot)\nabla_{x_{\parallel},v}G_{\pm}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}.

We split the integral region
B^{+}(x;t)
into two:
|x-y|<1
and
|x-y|\geq 1.
If
|x-y|\geq 1,
then the following inequality holds uniformly:

\langle t\rangle\leq\sqrt{2}\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|.

Therefore, we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{63}{8}m_{\pm}g\int_{B^{+}(x;t)\cap|x-y|\geq 1}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}^{2}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{63\sqrt{2}g}{2\beta m_{\pm}}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}\int_{B^{+}(x;t)\cap|x-y|\geq 1}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,v^{0}_{\pm}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{63\sqrt{2}g}{2\beta m_{\pm}}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}dye^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}m_{\pm}gy_{3}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\beta^{4}m_{\pm}^{2}}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta},

where
c_{\pm,\beta}
is defined as

c_{\pm,\beta}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,v^{0}_{\pm}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}v^{0}_{\pm}},

and satisfies
c_{\pm,\beta}\approx\beta^{-4}
by (
5.16
). On the other hand, if
|x-y|<1,
then we further make a change of variables
y\mapsto z\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}y-x
and then another change of variables to spherical coordinates
z\mapsto(r,\theta,\phi)
such that we have

\frac{63}{8}m_{\pm}g\int_{B^{+}(x;t)\cap|x-y|<1}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}^{2}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}\\
=\frac{63\pi}{4}m_{\pm}g\int_{0}^{\min\{1,t\}}dr\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi\ \sin\phi\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}^{2}}\frac{\langle t\rangle r}{\langle t-r\rangle}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}r|\sin\phi|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}g(r\cos\phi+x_{3}))}.

Using the inequality that

\langle t\rangle\leq\sqrt{2}\langle t-r\rangle\langle r\rangle,

we have

\frac{63\pi}{4}m_{\pm}g\int_{0}^{\min\{1,t\}}dr\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi\ \sin\phi\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}^{2}}\frac{\langle t\rangle r}{\langle t-r\rangle}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}r|\sin\phi|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}g(r\cos\phi+x_{3}))}\\
\leq\frac{63\sqrt{2}\pi g}{\beta m_{\pm}}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\int_{0}^{\min\{1,t\}}dr\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi\ \sin\phi\langle r\rangle^{2}\leq\frac{504\sqrt{2}\pi g}{\beta m_{\pm}}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}.

Altogether, we conclude that

(5.34)

\langle t\rangle|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1),\textup{acc}}_{\pm,iS}(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}}{\beta^{8}m_{\pm}^{2}}\left(1+m_{\pm}g\beta^{3}\right).

Choosing
\beta>1
sufficiently large such that
\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}\times\min\{g\beta^{3},\beta^{2}\}\gg 1
, we obtain

\langle t\rangle|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1),\textup{acc}}_{\pm,iS}(t,x)|\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g,

which ensures (
5.7
) for the decomposed piece
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1),\textup{acc}}_{\pm,iS}.
The other term
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(2),\textup{acc}}_{\pm,iS}
follows exactly the same estimate.

On the other hand, regarding the inhomogeneous stationary source term
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{\textup{st}}_{\pm,iS}
, we observe that

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1),\textup{st}}_{\pm,iS}(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq\langle t\rangle\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}\frac{dy}{|y-x|}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\ \left|\frac{\omega+\hat{v}_{\pm}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\right|\left|\left(\mathscr{E}^{l}+\hat{v}_{\pm}\times\mathscr{B}^{l}\right)\left(t-\frac{|x-y|}{c},y\right)\right||\nabla_{v}F_{\pm,\textup{st}}(y,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim m_{\pm}gC_{G_{\pm}}\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\beta(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})},

by the kernel estimate (
5.28
), decay of the momentum derivative of the stationary solution (
2.12
), and the uniform bounds for
\mathscr{E}^{l}
and
\mathscr{B}^{l}
in (
5.7
) where the constant
C_{G_{\pm}}
is defined as

C_{G_{\pm}}\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}C\|\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}(\cdot,0,\cdot)\nabla_{x_{\parallel},v}G_{\pm}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}.

Again, we split the integral region
B^{+}(x;t)
into two:
|x-y|<1
and
|x-y|\geq 1.
If
|x-y|\geq 1,
then the following inequality holds uniformly:

\langle t\rangle\leq\sqrt{2}\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|.

Therefore, we obtain that

\displaystyle m_{\pm}gC_{G_{\pm}}\int_{B^{+}(x;t)\cap|x-y|\geq 1}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\beta(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}

\displaystyle\lesssim gC_{G_{\pm}}\int_{B^{+}(x;t)\cap|x-y|\geq 1}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,v^{0}_{\pm}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\beta(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}

\displaystyle\lesssim gC_{G_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}dy\ e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\beta m_{\pm}gy_{3}}\lesssim\frac{C_{G_{\pm}}}{\beta^{3}m_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta},

where
c_{\pm,\beta}
is defined as
c_{\pm,\beta}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,v^{0}_{\pm}e^{-\beta v^{0}_{\pm}},
and satisfies
c_{\pm,\beta}\approx\beta^{-4}
by (
5.16
). On the other hand, if
|x-y|<1,
then we further make a change of variables
y\mapsto z\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}y-x
and then another change of variables to spherical coordinates
z\mapsto(r,\theta,\phi)
such that we have

m_{\pm}gC_{G_{\pm}}\int_{B^{+}(x;t)\cap|x-y|<1}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\beta(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}\\
\approx m_{\pm}gC_{G_{\pm}}\int_{0}^{\min\{1,t\}}dr\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi\ \sin\phi\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle r}{\langle t-r\rangle}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}r|\sin\phi|}e^{-\beta(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}g(r\cos\phi+x_{3}))}.

Using the inequality that

\langle t\rangle\leq\sqrt{2}\langle t-r\rangle\langle r\rangle,

we have

m_{\pm}gC_{G_{\pm}}\int_{0}^{\min\{1,t\}}dr\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi\ \sin\phi\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle r}{\langle t-r\rangle}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}r|\sin\phi|}e^{-\beta(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}g(r\cos\phi+x_{3}))}\\
\lesssim gC_{G_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\int_{0}^{\min\{1,t\}}dr\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi\ \sin\phi\langle r\rangle^{2}\lesssim gC_{G_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}.

Altogether, we conclude that

(5.35)

\langle t\rangle|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1),\textup{st}}_{\pm,iS}(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{C_{G_{\pm}}}{m_{\pm}\beta^{7}}\left(1+m_{\pm}g\beta^{3}\right).

Choosing
\beta>1
sufficiently large such that
\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}\times\min\{g\beta^{3},\beta^{2}\}\gg 1
, we obtain

\langle t\rangle|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1),\textup{st}}_{\pm,iS}(t,x)|\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g,

which ensures (
5.7
) for the decomposed piece
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1),\textup{st}}_{\pm,iS}.
The other term
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(2),\textup{st}}_{\pm,iS}
follows exactly the same estimate. This completes the estimate for
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})_{\pm,iS}.

5.2.5.
Decay Estimates for
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})_{\pm,iT}
and
(\mathscr{B}^{l+1})_{\pm,iT}

Recall that
\mathscr{E}^{l+1}_{\pm,T}
terms are written as

(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,T}(t,x)=\mp\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}\frac{dy}{|y-x|^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\ \frac{(|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{\pm}+\omega)}{(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)^{2}}f^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-|x-y|,y,v).

In the followings, we split the cases into two:
t<1
and
t\geq 1.

Firstly, if
t<1
, we utilize the estimate (
5.10
) and the kernel estimate (
5.26
) to obtain

(5.36)

\begin{split}|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,iT}(t,x)|&\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}\frac{dy}{|y-x|^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\ v^{0}_{\pm}f^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-|x-y|,y,v)\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}\frac{dy}{|y-x|^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\ v^{0}_{\pm}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}\\
&\qquad\times\left(\|\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}f^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\frac{C}{\beta}\|\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}(\cdot,0,\cdot)\nabla_{x_{\parallel},v}G_{\pm}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}\right)e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}\\
&\approx\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\left(\|\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}f^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\frac{C}{\beta}\|\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}(\cdot,0,\cdot)\nabla_{x_{\parallel},v}G_{\pm}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}\right)\\
&\qquad\times\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}\frac{dy}{|y-x|^{2}}e^{-\frac{m_{\pm}g\beta}{2}y_{3}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|},\end{split}

where
c_{\pm,\beta}
is defined as

c_{\pm,\beta}\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\ v^{0}_{\pm}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}v^{0}_{\pm}}.
Now we further split the integral domain into two:
|y-x|\leq 1
and
|y-x|>1.

If
|y-x|>1,
we have

\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}\frac{dy}{|y-x|^{2}}e^{-\frac{m_{\pm}g\beta}{2}y_{3}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}1_{\{|y-x|>1\}}\leq\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\int_{{\mathbb{R}^{3}}}dye^{-\frac{m_{\pm}g\beta}{2}y_{3}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{\pm}^{2}g\beta^{3}}c_{\pm,\beta}.

On the other hand, if
|y-x|\leq 1,
we further proceed as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}\frac{dy}{|y-x|^{2}}e^{-\frac{m_{\pm}g\beta}{2}y_{3}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}1_{\{|y-x|\leq 1\}}

\displaystyle\approx\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{z_{3}+x_{3}>0\}}\frac{dz}{|z|^{2}}e^{-\frac{m_{\pm}g\beta}{2}(z_{3}+x_{3})}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel}|}1_{\{|z|\leq 1\}}

\displaystyle\approx\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}\int_{0}^{1}dr\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}d\omega\ 1_{\{(r\omega)_{3}+x_{3}>0\}}e^{-\frac{m_{\pm}g\beta}{2}((r\omega)_{3}+x_{3})}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel}|}\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{\pm}}c_{\pm,\beta}.

Altogether, we conclude that for
i=1,2,3
,

(5.37)

|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,iT}(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{\pm}^{2}g\beta^{7}}(1+m_{\pm}g\beta^{3})\left(\|\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}f^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\frac{C}{\beta}\|\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}(\cdot,0,\cdot)\nabla_{x_{\parallel},v}G_{\pm}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}\right),

since we have the estimate (
5.16
) for the coefficient
c_{\pm,\beta}
.

On the other hand, if
t\geq 1,
by using the kernel estimate (
5.26
) and the decay estimate (
5.10
) for
f^{l+1}_{\pm}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,iT}(t,x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle f^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-|x-y|,y,v)}{|x-y|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{B^{+}(x;t)}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|^{2}}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{|y-x|<1}dy+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|y-x|\geq 1\\
|y-x|<t\end{subarray}}dy\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|^{2}}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{|y-x|<1}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-1\rangle|x-y|^{2}}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}

\displaystyle+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|y-x|\geq 1\\
|y-x|<t\end{subarray}}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{|y-x|<1}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{(1+\sqrt{5})}{2|x-y|^{2}}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}

\displaystyle+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|y-x|\geq 1\\
|y-x|<t\end{subarray}}dy\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dv\,\frac{v^{0}_{\pm}}{m_{\pm}}\frac{\langle t\rangle}{\langle t-|x-y|\rangle|x-y|}\frac{4}{\beta}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}e^{-\frac{\beta}{2}|y_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\beta}{4}(v^{0}_{\pm}+m_{\pm}gy_{3})}\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\text{I}+\text{II},

where
C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}
is defined as (
5.2.4
). Here note that the integrand of the latter integral II is the same as that of (
5.33
) up to some constant and
g
. Therefore, the same estimate follows, and by (
5.34
) we obtain

\text{II}\lesssim C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}\left(\frac{1}{\beta^{8}m_{\pm}^{2}g}+\frac{1}{\beta^{5}m_{\pm}}\right).

On the other hand, the integral I can be treated the same as the integral (
5.36
) up to a minor correction on the coefficients, and hence the estimate (
5.37
) follows as

\text{I}\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{\pm}g\beta^{4}}C_{f_{\pm}^{\textup{in}},G_{\pm}}.

Altogether, choosing
\beta>0
sufficiently large, we obtain

\langle t\rangle|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,iT}(t,x)|\ll\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g,

which ensures (
5.7
) for the decomposed piece
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,iT}.
The other term
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1})^{(2)}_{\pm,iT}
follows exactly the same estimate. In addition,
in order to conclude the same upper bound for the magnetic field
(\mathscr{B}^{l+1})^{(1)}_{\pm,iT}
and
(\mathscr{B}^{l+1})^{(2)}_{\pm,iT}
up to constant, we now make some kernel estimates as follows. We first note that

\frac{(1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2})(\omega\times(\hat{v}_{\pm}))_{i}}{(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)^{2}}=\frac{(1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2})(\omega_{1}(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{2}-\omega_{2}(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{1})}{(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)^{2}}.

By (
5.22
), we first have

\left|\frac{1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\right|\leq 2.

Now, for the estimate of the remainder part
\frac{\omega_{1}(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{2}-\omega_{2}(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{1}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}
,
define
z\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}-\frac{(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}
such that

\omega\cdot z=-\frac{\omega\cdot(\hat{v}_{\pm})}{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|}=\cos\phi.

Similarly to what we did in (
5.23
), we observe that

\left|\frac{(\omega\times(\hat{v}_{\pm}))_{i}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\right|\leq\frac{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})||\sin\phi|}{|1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi|}.

Define
f(\sin\phi)=\frac{|\sin\phi|}{|1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\cos\phi|}
. Then by (
5.24
), we obtain
f(x)\leq\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}.

Thus,

(5.38)

\left|\frac{(\omega\times(\hat{v}_{\pm}))_{i}}{1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega}\right|\leq|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}.

Altogether, we have

(5.39)

\left|\frac{(1-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|^{2})(\omega\times(\hat{v}_{\pm}))_{i}}{(1+\hat{v}_{\pm}\cdot\omega)^{2}}\right|\leq 2|(\hat{v}_{\pm})|\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}\leq 2\frac{\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v|^{2}}}{m_{\pm}}.

5.2.6.
Final Upper-Bounds for
\mathscr{E}^{l+1}
and
\mathscr{B}^{l+1}

Combining the previous estimates, we obtain the following lemma on the linear-in-time decay upper bound for
\mathscr{E}^{l+1}
and
\mathscr{B}^{l+1}
:

Lemma 5.6
.

Fix
l\in\mathbb{N}
and suppose (
5.6
)-(
5.7
) hold for
(f^{l+1}_{\pm},\mathscr{E}^{l},\mathscr{B}^{l}).
Then
(\mathscr{E}^{l+1},\mathscr{B}^{l+1})
satisfies

(5.40)

\sup_{t\geq 0}\ \langle t\rangle\|(\mathscr{E}^{l+1},\mathscr{B}^{l+1})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\min\{m_{+},m_{-}\}\frac{g}{16}.

This bound guarantees the validity of (
5.7
) at the
(l+1)
-th iteration level, provided that the parameter
\beta>1
is chosen sufficiently large. Consequently, the estimates (
5.6
)-(
5.7
) are verified uniformly for all
l\in\mathbb{N}
, and thus remain valid in the limit as
l\to\infty
.

6.
Regularity Estimates for the Distributions

This section is devoted to establishing regularity estimates for the iterated sequence of solutions
(F^{l+1}_{\pm},

\mathbf{E}^{l+1},

\mathbf{B}^{l+1})
to (
4.6
)–(
4.7
), (
5.3
)–(
5.4
) and (
5.1
)–(
5.2
). We prove that
(F^{l+1}_{\pm},\mathbf{E}^{l+1},\mathbf{B}^{l+1})
possess sufficient time and space regularity in appropriate weak function spaces. More precisely, we show that

F^{l+1}_{\pm}\in W^{1,\infty}\left([0,T];L^{\infty}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\right)\cap L^{\infty}\left([0,T];X(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\right),\text{ and }

(\mathbf{E}^{l+1},\mathbf{B}^{l+1})\in W^{1,\infty}_{t,x}([0,T]\times\Omega)\times W^{1,\infty}_{t,x}([0,T]\times\Omega),

where
X
is a weighted first-order derivative space for
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
. In addition, we prove that the temporal and momentum derivatives of
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
exhibit sufficient decay in
x
and
v
, controlled within suitable weighted Sobolev spaces.

Given
l\in\mathbb{N}
, we interpret the given fields in the iterated equation (
5.3
) as
\mathbf{E}^{l},\mathbf{B}^{l}
at the level of the sequential index
(l)
, while the trajectory
\mathscr{Z}_{\pm}^{l+1}=(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm},\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm})
is understood at the level of
(l+1)
, defined via the fields
\mathbf{E}^{l},\mathbf{B}^{l}
as in (
5.5
). In this section, we study the derivative estimates of the distribution
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
, and in Section
7
, we study the derivatives of the fields
\mathbf{E}^{l+1},\mathbf{B}^{l+1}
at the level of
(l+1)
. Notice that the final upper-bound estimates for the derivatives-(
6.26
) for
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
and (
7.1
) for
\mathbf{E}^{l+1},\mathbf{B}^{l+1}
-are uniform in
l
, ensuring that these bounds are preserved in the limit as
l\to\infty
. For the rest of Section
6
and Section
7
we keep the same iterated sequence elements
(F^{l+1}_{\pm},\mathbf{E}^{l+1},\mathbf{B}^{l+1})
of Section
5
.

Denote the given forcing term in the linear Vlasov equation (
5.3
) as

(6.1)

\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}(t,x,v)\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\pm\mathbf{E}^{l}(t,x)\pm(\hat{v}_{\pm})\times\mathbf{B}^{l}(t,x)-m_{\pm}g\hat{e}_{3}.

6.1.
Derivatives of the Distribution

Given the system (
3.38
) of ordinary differential equations for the characteristic trajectories
\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}
and
\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}
, we can now write the representations of the derivatives of the distribution function
F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)
using the solution representation (
3.43
).

6.1.1.
Temporal Derivative
\partial_{t}F^{l+1}_{\pm}

The temporal derivative
\partial_{t}F^{l+1}_{\pm}
of a distribution
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
will be estimated via the Vlasov equation (
1.1
)
1
using the given estimates for
(\mathbf{E}^{l},\mathbf{B}^{l})
and the estimates for
\nabla_{x}F^{l+1}_{\pm}
and
\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}
obtained below.

6.1.2.
Position Derivative
\nabla_{x}F^{l+1}_{\pm}

For the position derivative, we again need to consider the two cases:

t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)
and
t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)
.

If
t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)
observe that

\nabla_{x}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)=\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)\\
+\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v).

On the other hand, if
t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)
,
we have

\nabla_{x}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)=(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{x}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}+(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{x}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}.

6.1.3.
Momentum Derivative
\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}

For the momentum derivative, if
t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)

we have

\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)=\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)\\
+\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v).

On the other hand, if
t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)

we have

\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)\\
=(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{v}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}\\
+(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{v}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}.

Thus, the derivatives of the distribution function
F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)
with respect to
t
,
x
, and
v
can be collected as follows:

(6.2)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)

\displaystyle=-(\hat{v}_{\pm})\cdot\nabla_{x}F^{l+1}_{\pm}-\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}\cdot\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm},\textup{ where we further represent $\nabla_{x}F^{l+1}_{\pm}$ and $\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}$ by}

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)\\
\quad+\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\ \textup{if }t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v),\\
(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{x}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}+(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{x}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1},\ \textup{if }t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v),\end{cases}

\displaystyle\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)\\
\quad+\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\ \textup{if }t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v),\\
(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{v}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}+(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{v}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1},\ \textup{if }t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v).\end{cases}

Observe that the representations above still contain derivatives, gradients, and Jacobian matrices of
t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
,
x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}
, and
v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}.

To begin with, for the representation of
\partial_{t}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
, we recall that

(6.3)

(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v);t,x,v)=0,

by definition of
t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v).

By differentiating (
6.3
) with respect to
t,
we have

(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})(1-\partial_{t}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})+\partial_{t}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})=0.

Thus, we have

(6.4)

\partial_{t}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}=1+\frac{\partial_{t}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})}{(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})}.

Similarly, by differentiating (
6.3
) with respect to
x_{i}
and
v_{i}
for
i=1,2,3,
we obtain

-(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})\partial_{x_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}+\partial_{x_{i}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})=0,\text{ and }

-(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})\partial_{v_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}+\partial_{v_{i}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})=0.

Therefore, we have

(6.5)

\partial_{x_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}=\frac{\partial_{x_{i}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})}{(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})}\textup{ and }\partial_{v_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}=\frac{\partial_{v_{i}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})}{(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})},

for
i=1,2,3.

Regarding the Jacobian matrices of
x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}
and
v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}
, we observe that

(6.6)

\begin{split}\partial_{x_{i}}x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}&=\partial_{x_{i}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v))=-\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v)\partial_{x_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}+(\partial_{x_{i}}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v),\\
\partial_{v_{i}}x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}&=\partial_{v_{i}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v))=-\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v)\partial_{v_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}+(\partial_{v_{i}}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v),\\
\partial_{x_{i}}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}&=\partial_{x_{i}}(\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v))=-(\partial_{x_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})+(\partial_{x_{i}}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm})(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v),\\
\partial_{v_{i}}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}&=\partial_{v_{i}}(\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v))=-(\partial_{v_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})+(\partial_{v_{i}}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm})(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}};t,x,v),\end{split}

for
i=1,2,3
by (
3.38
). This completes the representations of the derivatives of
t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
,
x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}
, and
v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}
with respect to
t,x,
and
v.

We also need the derivatives of the characteristic trajectory variables
\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}
and
\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}
. We first collect several preliminary derivative estimates on the trajectories in the following lemma.

Lemma 6.1
.

Define
\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}
as (
6.1
).
Suppose that

(6.7)

\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq T}\|\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{E}^{l},\mathbf{B}^{l})(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{1},\text{ and }\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq T}\|\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{2},

for some
C_{1},C_{2}>0.
Then for any
s,t\in(0,T)
we have

(6.8)

|\nabla_{x}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)|,\ |\nabla_{x}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)|,\ (v^{0}_{\pm})|\nabla_{v}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)|,\ |\nabla_{v}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)|\lesssim_{T}1,

where we denote
\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)=\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
and
\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)=\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
. If
i,j=1,2,3
and
i\neq j
, then we can further have

(6.9)

(v^{0}_{\pm})|(\partial_{x_{i}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{j})(s)|\lesssim_{T}1.

Thus, we also have
for
i=1,2,3,

(6.10)

\begin{split}|\partial_{x_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}|&\lesssim_{T}\frac{1}{|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|},\quad|\partial_{v_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}|\lesssim_{T}\frac{1}{|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|}\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{v^{0}_{\pm}},\\
|\partial_{x_{i}}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}|&\lesssim|\partial_{x_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}|+|(\partial_{x_{i}}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{\parallel}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|,\quad|\partial_{x_{i}}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{3}|\lesssim_{T}1,\\
|\partial_{v_{i}}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}|&\lesssim|\partial_{v_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}|+|(\partial_{v_{i}}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{\parallel}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|,\quad|\partial_{v_{i}}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{3}|\lesssim_{T}\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{v^{0}_{\pm}},\\
|\partial_{x_{i}}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}|&\lesssim_{T}\left(\frac{1}{|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|}+1\right),\quad|\partial_{v_{i}}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}|\lesssim_{T}\left(\frac{1}{|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|}+1\right)\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{v^{0}_{\pm}}.\end{split}

For
i=1,2
, we can further have

(6.11)

\begin{split}|\partial_{x_{i}}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}|&\lesssim_{T}\frac{1}{|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|}\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{v^{0}_{\pm}},\quad|\partial_{x_{i}}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{3}|\lesssim_{T}\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{v^{0}_{\pm}},\\
|\partial_{x_{i}}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}|&\lesssim_{T}\left(\frac{1}{|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|}+1\right)\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{v^{0}_{\pm}}.\end{split}

Proof.

The estimates (
6.8
) and (
6.9
) are obtained from
[
5
, Lemma 11]
. The remaining estimates, (
6.10
) and (
6.11
), then follow from (
6.5
) and (
6.6
). We omit the details.
∎

6.2.
First-Round Estimate

This section is devoted to obtaining a global-in-time uniform upper-bound estimate for the derivatives of
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
. Note that the representation (
3.43
) of
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
consists of the initial-value part and the boundary-value part, and the derivatives on
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
involves the derivatives of the characteristics
\nabla_{x}\mathscr{Z}_{\pm}
and hence
\nabla_{x}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
. As we have already observed in (
6.5
), the derivatives of
t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
contains possible singularity on
(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})
and we have to handle this singularity to obtain a derivative estimate for
f.
To this end, we define the following kinetic-type weights:

Definition 6.2
.

Define the kinetic weight

(6.12)

\alpha_{\pm}(t,x,v)=\sqrt{x_{3}^{2}+|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|^{2}-2\left((\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v)\right)\frac{x_{3}}{(v^{0}_{\pm})}}.

This weight is well-defined as long as
-(\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm})_{3}>0.
Note that

(6.13)

\alpha_{\pm}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v)=|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|.

In addition, we define a special weight in the form of

(6.14)

\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}^{2}(t,x,v)\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\frac{\alpha_{\pm}^{2}(t,x,v)}{1+\alpha_{\pm}^{2}(t,x,v)}.

This special weight
\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}^{2}
is uniformly bounded from above by 1 and is small when
\alpha_{\pm}
is small. One crucial property of
\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}
is on the fact that it vanishes at the grazing point
(x_{3},v_{3})=(0,0)
as

(6.15)

\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v)=\frac{|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|}{\sqrt{1+|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|^{2}}}.

Remark 6.3
.

We note that the form of the weight (
6.14
) differs slightly from the classical kinetic weight
\alpha
introduced in
[
13
]
, as well as from the variant employed in
[
5
]
.
In particular, even for large values of
\alpha
, the weight
\tilde{\alpha}
remains uniformly bounded by
1
.

We first study the upper-bound estimates of
\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}^{2}
along the characteristic trajectory.
We introduce the following velocity lemma. The velocity lemma is originally established in
[
13
]
.

Lemma 6.4

(Velocity Lemma)
.

Let
\alpha_{\pm}
and
\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}
be defined as in (
6.12
) and (
6.14
), respectively. Define
\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}
as (
6.1
). Suppose

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigg(\|\mathbf{E}^{l}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\mathbf{B}^{l}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(\partial_{t},\nabla_{x})\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigg)<C.

Suppose that for all
(t,x_{\parallel})\in(0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{2},

-(\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)>c_{0},
for some
c_{0}>0.
Then for any
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\Omega\times{\mathbb{R}^{3}},
with the trajectory
\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
and
\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
satisfying (
3.38
),

(6.16)

e^{-10\frac{C}{c_{0}}|t-s|}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)\leq\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(s,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v))\leq e^{10\frac{C}{c_{0}}|t-s|}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)

In addition, define the material derivative

\frac{D}{Dt}\overset{\mbox{\tiny{def}}}{=}\partial_{t}+(\hat{v}_{\pm})\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}\cdot\nabla_{v}.
Then we have

(6.17)

\left|\frac{D}{Dt}\alpha_{\pm}^{2}\right|\leq 20\frac{C}{c_{0}}\alpha_{\pm}^{2}.

Proof.

We first observe that

\frac{D}{Dt}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}^{2}=\frac{1}{1+\alpha_{\pm}^{2}}\frac{D}{Dt}\alpha_{\pm}^{2}-\frac{\alpha_{\pm}^{2}}{(1+\alpha_{\pm}^{2})^{2}}\frac{D}{Dt}\alpha_{\pm}^{2}=\frac{1}{(1+\alpha_{\pm}^{2})^{2}}\frac{D}{Dt}\alpha_{\pm}^{2}.

Then using the bound (
6.17
) of the material derivative
\frac{D}{Dt}\alpha_{\pm}^{2}
we further obtain

\frac{D}{Dt}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}^{2}\leq 20\frac{C}{c_{0}(1+\alpha_{\pm}^{2})}\frac{\alpha_{\pm}^{2}}{1+\alpha_{\pm}^{2}}\leq 20\frac{C}{c_{0}}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}^{2}.

By the Grönwall lemma, we finally obtain

\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}^{2}(s,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s))\leq e^{\frac{20C}{c_{0}}|t-s|}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}^{2}(t,x,v).

This completes the proof of Lemma
6.4
. Lastly, the proof of (
6.17
) follows by
[
5
, Eq. (4.10)]
with
E_{e}=B_{e}=0
and
C_{1}=C.

∎

Now we will prove that this weight
\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}
also satisfies the following crucial property that if
t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},

t\lesssim\sup_{t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)|^{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(0,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)).

To prove this, we first need to obtain the following prerequisite lemma.

Lemma 6.5
.

For
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\Omega\times{\mathbb{R}^{3}},
let the trajectory
\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
and
\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
satisfy (
3.38
). Suppose for all
t,x,v
,

(6.18)

-(\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v)>c_{0},

for some
c_{0}>0
. Then if
t<t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},
we have

(6.19)

|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|^{2}\leq\frac{2\alpha_{\pm}^{2}(t,x,v)}{1+\alpha_{\pm}^{2}(t,x,v)}.

Proof.

Note that since we have (
6.18
), we first observe from the definition of
\alpha_{\pm}
in (
6.12
) that
\alpha_{\pm}^{2}(t,x,v)\geq|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|^{2}.
Since
|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|\leq 1,
we obtain

|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|^{2}\leq\alpha_{\pm}^{2}(t,x,v)\leq(2-|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|^{2})\alpha_{\pm}^{2}(t,x,v).

This provides the final conclusion (
6.19
).
∎

As a corollary of Lemma
6.4
and Lemma
6.5
, we can prove the following crucial lemma.

Lemma 6.6
.

For
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\Omega\times{\mathbb{R}^{3}},
let the trajectory
\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
and
\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
satisfy (
3.38
). Suppose for all
t,x,v
, assume (
6.18
) for some
c_{0}>0
, then there exists a constant
C
depending on
T,

g,

\|\mathbf{E}^{l}|_{W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega)},
and
\|\mathbf{B}^{l}\|_{W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega)},
such that if
t<t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},
then

(6.20)

t<\max\left\{\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)\rangle,(v^{0}_{\pm})\right\}\frac{\sqrt{2}}{c_{0}}\left(1+e^{\frac{10CT}{c_{0}}}\right)\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(0,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)).

Furthermore, if
t<t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
and
s\in(0,\min\{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},T\})
then

(6.21)

|t-s|<\max\left\{\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)\rangle,(v^{0}_{\pm})\right\}\frac{\sqrt{2}}{c_{0}}\left(1+e^{\frac{10C|t-s|}{c_{0}}}\right)\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(s,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)).

Proof.

For
t<t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},
we observe that

\int_{0}^{t}c_{0}ds<\int_{0}^{t}-(\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm})_{3}(s,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s))ds=(\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(0)-v_{3}.

Thus, we have

c_{0}t<|(\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(0)|+|v_{3}|\leq\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)\rangle|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(0)|+(v^{0}_{\pm})|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|\\
\leq\max\left\{\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)\rangle,(v^{0}_{\pm})\right\}(|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(0)|+|(\hat{v}_{\pm})_{3}|).

Now we use (
6.19
) and further obtain

c_{0}t<\max\left\{\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)\rangle,(v^{0}_{\pm})\right\}\sqrt{2}(\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(0,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0))+\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)).

Finally, using (
6.16
), we obtain

c_{0}t<\max\left\{\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)\rangle,(v^{0}_{\pm})\right\}\sqrt{2}\left(1+e^{\frac{10Ct}{c_{0}}}\right)\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(0,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)).

This completes the proof of Lemma
6.20
.
∎

On the other hand, if
t\geq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},
we introduce the following bound on the singularity
\frac{1}{|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}|}.

Lemma 6.7

(Lemma 10 of
[
5
]
)
.

For
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\Omega\times{\mathbb{R}^{3}},
let the trajectory
\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
and
\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s;t,x,v)
satisfy (
3.38
). Suppose for all
t,x,v
,
-(\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v)>c_{0},
then there exists a constant
C
depending on
T,

g,

\|\mathbf{E}^{l}\|_{W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega)},
and
\|\mathbf{B}^{l}|_{W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega)},
such that if
t\geq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},

(6.22)

\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)}{(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})}\leq\frac{C}{c_{0}}\max_{s\in\{t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},t\}}\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)|^{2}}.

6.3.
Enhanced Estimates of the Momentum Derivatives

By compensating for some loss of decay from the initial and boundary profiles (cf. Section
4.3.1
in the stationary case), we can further prove some additional decay-in-
(x,v)
estimates for the momentum derivatives of
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
. This additional decay will be crucial for the uniform estimates on the temporal derivatives of the electromagnetic fields
(\mathbf{E}^{l+1},\mathbf{B}^{l+1})
, which will be used for the uniform estimates on
x_{3}
-derivatives. To this end, we will prove the following decay estimates of the momentum derivatives:

Proposition 6.8
.

Suppose the same assumptions made in Proposition
6.9
.
In addition, suppose that the initial profile
F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}
and the incoming boundary profile
G_{\pm}
further satisfies the following fast-decay condition on the first-order derivative in the velocity variable:

(6.23)

\|\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)\nabla_{x,v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}(x_{\parallel},0,v)\nabla_{x_{\parallel},v}G_{\pm}(x_{\parallel},v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}<\infty,

where the weight
\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}
is defined as in (
3.46
).
Given (
6.23
), we will prove the following estimate on a sequential level; for each
l\in\mathbb{N},
we have in
\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}_{+},

(6.24)

\|\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C\left(\|\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)\nabla_{x,v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}(x_{\parallel},0,v)\nabla_{x_{\parallel},v}G_{\pm}(x_{\parallel},v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}\right),

for some
C>0
.

Proof.

By taking the momentum derivative on
F^{l+1}_{\pm}
, we obtain as in (
6.2
)

\displaystyle\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)

\displaystyle=1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)}\bigg(\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)

\displaystyle+\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)\bigg)

\displaystyle+1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)}\bigg((\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{v}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}+(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{v}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}\bigg).

Then we note that the derivatives of
\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm},

\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}
,
x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}
and
v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}
satisfy the upper-bounds estimates (
6.8
) and (
6.10
). Therefore, using (
6.8
) and (
6.10
), we observe that

\displaystyle|\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)|(\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\nabla_{v}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)|

\displaystyle+\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)|(\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\nabla_{v}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)|

\displaystyle+\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})||\nabla_{v}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}|+\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)|(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})||\nabla_{v}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)\bigg(|(\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))|+|(\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))|

\displaystyle+\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)\bigg(|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})|\left(\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{|(\hat{V}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|(v^{0}_{\pm})}+\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{v^{0}_{\pm}}\right)

\displaystyle+|(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})|\bigg|\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{|(\hat{V}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|(v^{0}_{\pm})}+(v^{0}_{\pm})^{-1}\bigg|\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)}\left(\frac{\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)}{\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v),x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}(t,x,v),v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}(t,x,v))}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\bigg(|(\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))|+|(\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))|

\displaystyle+|(\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|+|(\mathrm{w}^{2}_{\pm,\beta}\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})|\bigg),

by Lemma
6.7
. Then we further use the weight comparison (
3.57
) and observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)}\left(\frac{\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}\left(\mathscr{Z}^{l+1}_{\pm}\left(t;t,x,v\right)\right)}{\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(\mathscr{Z}^{l+1}_{\pm}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v);t,x,v))}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\mathrm{w}_{\pm,\beta}(x,v)}e^{\left(\left\|\mathbf{E}^{l}\right\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}+1\right)\frac{16\beta}{5m_{\pm}g}(\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v_{\pm}|^{2}}+m_{\pm}gx_{3})}

\displaystyle\leq e^{(\min\{m_{-},m_{+}\}g)\left(\frac{1}{8}+\frac{1}{32}\right)\frac{16\beta}{5m_{\pm}g}(\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v_{\pm}|^{2}}+m_{\pm}gx_{3})}e^{-\beta v_{\pm}^{0}-m_{\pm}g\beta x_{3}-\frac{\beta}{2}|x_{\parallel}|}

\displaystyle\leq e^{\frac{\beta}{2}(\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|v_{\pm}|^{2}}+m_{\pm}gx_{3})}e^{-\beta v_{\pm}^{0}-m_{\pm}g\beta x_{3}-\frac{\beta}{2}|x_{\parallel}|}\leq 1,

given that
\mathbf{E}^{l}
satisfies the upper-bound (
4.18
) and (
5.7
) and that

\min\{m_{+},m_{-}\}g\geq 32.
This proves the decay estimate (
6.24
) for the momentum derivative
\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}.

This completes the proof.

∎

We close this section by introducing uniform-boundedness estimates on the derivatives.

Proposition 6.9
.

Fix
m>4.
Define
\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}
as (
6.1
). Suppose that the initial-boundary data satisfy

\displaystyle\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\partial_{x_{3}}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}

\displaystyle<\infty,

\displaystyle\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{v}G_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}

\displaystyle<\infty.

Consider the corresponding solution sequence
(F^{l}_{\pm},\mathbf{E}^{l},\mathbf{B}^{l})_{l\in\mathbb{N}}
associated to the initial-boundary data
F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}
and
G_{\pm}
.
Suppose further that

(6.25)

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{E}^{l}(t),\mathbf{B}^{l}(t))\|_{L^{\infty}}<C_{1}\text{ and }\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}}<C_{2},

for some
T>0
,
C_{1}>0
and
C_{2}>0.
Suppose that
-(\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v)>c_{0},
for
t\in[0,T]
,
x_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and
v\in{\mathbb{R}^{3}}.

Then we have

(6.26)

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\partial_{t}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{l+1}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\partial_{x_{3}}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}F^{l+1}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{v}F^{l+1}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}})\\
\leq C_{T},

for some constant
C_{T}>0
which depends only on
C_{1},C_{2},T,

F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}
and
G_{\pm}
.

Remark 6.10
.

The derivative estimate (
6.26
) is uniform in
l
, and hence the limit
F_{\pm}^{\infty}
also satisfies the same estimate.

Proof.

Fix
m>4
. By (
6.2
), we observe that
(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{l+1}_{\pm}|
is bounded from above by

\begin{split}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|&\leq(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\bigg|(\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)\\
&+(\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)\bigg|1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)}\\
&+(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\bigg|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}\\
&+(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}\bigg|1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)}\\
&\lesssim 1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{\parallel}(0;t,x,v)|\\
&+1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|(\partial_{x_{3}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(0;t,x,v)|\\
&+1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|(\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)|\\
&+1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})||\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}|\\
&+1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})||\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}|.\end{split}

In general, notice that

\sup_{t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)\rangle\lesssim\sup_{t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\left(1+|\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)|+\bigg|\int_{0}^{s}d\tau\ \mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}(\tau,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(\tau),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(\tau))\bigg|\right)\\
\lesssim\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)\rangle+C_{2}\max\{T,|t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}|\},

by (
6.25
).
In addition, note that for
0\leq s\leq T,

(6.27)

(v^{0}_{\pm})\lesssim\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)\rangle+\bigg|\int_{s}^{t}d\tau\ \mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}(\tau,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(\tau),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(\tau))\bigg|\lesssim\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)\rangle+C_{2}T\lesssim C_{T}\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)\rangle,

by (
6.25
).
Using (
6.27
), (
6.8
), and (
6.9
) with
s=0
and (
6.20
) for
t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
terms and using (
6.10
)–(
6.11
) for
t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
terms, we obtain

\displaystyle(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{T}\bigg(\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}\partial_{x_{3}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+C_{T}\bigg(1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})|\bigg|\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{|\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|(v^{0}_{\pm})}+1\bigg|

\displaystyle+1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}|(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})|\bigg|\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{|\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|(v^{0}_{\pm})}+1\bigg|\bigg),

where also used
t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}\leq T
for the terms with
1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}.

For the terms with
1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}},
by Lemma
6.7
, we further observe that if
t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
,

(6.28)

\bigg|\frac{t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{|(\hat{\mathscr{V}}_{\pm}^{l+1})_{3}(t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}})|(v^{0}_{\pm})}\bigg|\leq\frac{C}{c_{0}}\frac{\max_{s\in\{t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}},t\}}\sqrt{m_{\pm}^{2}+|\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(s)|^{2}}}{(v^{0}_{\pm})}\\
\lesssim\frac{C}{c_{0}}\sup_{t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\left(1+\frac{1}{(v^{0}_{\pm})}\bigg|\int_{s}^{t}\mathscr{F}^{l}_{\pm}(\tau,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(\tau),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(\tau))d\tau\bigg|\right)\lesssim\frac{C}{c_{0}}\left(1+\frac{C_{2}t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}{(v^{0}_{\pm})}\right)\lesssim C_{T}.

Also for
1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}
terms, we use (
6.27
) with
s=t-t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}
and that
t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}<t\leq T
to conclude that

\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim C_{T}\bigg(\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}\partial_{x_{3}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\bigg)\\
+C_{T}\bigg(\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}+\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\nabla_{v}G_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x_{\parallel},v}}\bigg).

Regarding the derivative
\partial_{x_{3}}F^{l+1}_{\pm},
we observe that (
6.2
) implies

\displaystyle(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|\partial_{x_{3}}F^{l+1}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|

\displaystyle\leq(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)\bigg|(\nabla_{x}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\partial_{x_{3}}\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)

\displaystyle+(\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))\cdot\partial_{x_{3}}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)\bigg|1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)}

\displaystyle+(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)\bigg|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\partial_{x_{3}}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}+(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})\cdot\partial_{x_{3}}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}\bigg|1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\partial_{x_{3}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{\parallel}(0;t,x,v)|

\displaystyle+1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|(\partial_{x_{3}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\partial_{x_{3}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(0;t,x,v)|

\displaystyle+1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|(\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\partial_{x_{3}}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)|

\displaystyle+1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})||\partial_{x_{3}}(x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel}|

\displaystyle+1_{t>t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)|(\nabla_{v}G_{\pm})((x_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})_{\parallel},v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1})||\partial_{x_{3}}v_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}^{l+1}|.

For the terms with
1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}
, we use (
6.9
) with
i=3
,
j=1,2
and
s=0
for
\partial_{x_{3}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{\parallel}(0)
term and use (
6.8
) with
i=3
and
s=0
for
\partial_{x_{3}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(0)
and
\partial_{x_{3}}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)
terms to further obtain that

(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\partial_{x_{3}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{\parallel}(0;t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim C_{T}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m-1}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))|,

(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\partial_{x_{3}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\partial_{x_{3}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(0;t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim C_{T}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\partial_{x_{3}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))|,

(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\partial_{x_{3}}\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim C_{T}(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))|,

since
t\leq T.

Therefore, by the fact that
\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}\leq 1
and that
\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}
also satisfies the additional bound (
6.16
) with
s=0,
we have

(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\partial_{x_{3}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{\parallel}(0;t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim C_{T}\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)\rangle^{m-1}1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))|\lesssim C_{T}\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m-1}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\partial_{x_{3}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\partial_{x_{3}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(0;t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim C_{T}\langle\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0)\rangle^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(0,\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0))1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\partial_{x_{3}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))|\\
\lesssim C_{T}\|(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}\partial_{x_{3}}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

(v^{0}_{\pm})^{m}\tilde{\alpha}_{\pm}(t,x,v)1_{t\leq t^{l+1}_{\pm,\mathbf{b}}}|(\nabla_{v}F^{\textup{in}}_{\pm})(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v),\mathscr{V}^{l+1}_{\pm}(0;t,x,v))||\partial_{x_{3}}(\mathscr{X}^{l+1}_{\pm})_{3}(0;t,x,v)|\\
